FILE: src/canvas/types.ts

/**
 * Canvas IR (Intermediate Representation) v1
 * 
 * Represents a document as a set of pages, each containing elements with
 * exact 2D coordinates (x, y, width, height, z-index), styling, typography,
 * and media resources.
 */

// ============================================================================
// 1. Coordinate & Unit Conversions
// ============================================================================

/**
 * Standard unit conversions:
 * Internal unit is PostScript point (pt, 1/72 inch).
 * 
 * - 1 pt = 20 twips (dxa)
 * - 1 pt = 12,700 EMUs (English Metric Units)
 * - 1 pt = 100 HWPUs (Hancom Word Processor Units)
 * - 1 pt = 25.4 / 72 mm ≈ 0.352778 mm
 * - 1 mm = 72 / 25.4 pt ≈ 2.834646 pt
 * - 1 CSS px (96 DPI) = 72 / 96 pt = 0.75 pt
 */

export function ptToTwip(pt: number): number {
  return Math.round(pt * 20);
}

export function twipToPt(twip: number): number {
  return twip / 20;
}

export function ptToEmu(pt: number): number {
  return Math.round(pt * 12700);
}

export function emuToPt(emu: number): number {
  return emu / 12700;
}

export function ptToHwpu(pt: number): number {
  return Math.round(pt * 100);
}

export function hwpuToPt(hwpu: number): number {
  return hwpu / 100;
}

export function ptToMm(pt: number): number {
  return (pt * 25.4) / 72;
}

export function mmToPt(mm: number): number {
  return (mm * 72) / 25.4;
}

export function figmaPxToPt(px: number, dpi: number = 96): number {
  return (px * 72) / dpi;
}

// ============================================================================
// 2. Scale Policy
// ============================================================================

export type ScalePolicy = "css-96dpi" | "fit-a4" | "1:1";

export interface ResolvedScale {
  scaleX: number;
  scaleY: number;
  targetWidthPt: number;
  targetHeightPt: number;
  policy: ScalePolicy;
}

/**
 * Resolves scaling from Figma frame dimensions to output canvas dimensions.
 * Standard A4 portrait in pt: 595.28 x 841.89 pt (210 x 297 mm).
 */
export function resolveScale(
  frameWidthPx: number,
  frameHeightPx: number,
  policy: ScalePolicy = "fit-a4"
): ResolvedScale {
  const A4_WIDTH_PT = 595.28;
  const A4_HEIGHT_PT = 841.89;

  if (policy === "1:1") {
    return {
      scaleX: 1.0,
      scaleY: 1.0,
      targetWidthPt: frameWidthPx,
      targetHeightPt: frameHeightPx,
      policy,
    };
  }

  if (policy === "css-96dpi") {
    const scale = 0.75; // 72 / 96
    return {
      scaleX: scale,
      scaleY: scale,
      targetWidthPt: frameWidthPx * scale,
      targetHeightPt: frameHeightPx * scale,
      policy,
    };
  }

  // policy === 'fit-a4'
  // If frame aspect ratio is close to A4 (0.68 ~ 0.73), scale to fit exact A4 width/height
  const ratio = frameWidthPx / frameHeightPx;
  const isA4Portrait = ratio >= 0.65 && ratio <= 0.75;
  const isA4Landscape = ratio >= 1.35 && ratio <= 1.50;

  if (isA4Portrait) {
    const scaleX = A4_WIDTH_PT / frameWidthPx;
    const scaleY = A4_HEIGHT_PT / frameHeightPx;
    return {
      scaleX,
      scaleY,
      targetWidthPt: A4_WIDTH_PT,
      targetHeightPt: A4_HEIGHT_PT,
      policy,
    };
  }

  if (isA4Landscape) {
    const scaleX = A4_HEIGHT_PT / frameWidthPx;
    const scaleY = A4_WIDTH_PT / frameHeightPx;
    return {
      scaleX,
      scaleY,
      targetWidthPt: A4_HEIGHT_PT,
      targetHeightPt: A4_WIDTH_PT,
      policy,
    };
  }

  // Fallback to standard 96 DPI CSS conversion
  const scale = 0.75;
  return {
    scaleX: scale,
    scaleY: scale,
    targetWidthPt: frameWidthPx * scale,
    targetHeightPt: frameHeightPx * scale,
    policy,
  };
}

// ============================================================================
// 3. Canvas IR Data Model
// ============================================================================

export interface CanvasBounds {
  x: number; // pt, relative to page top-left origin (0, 0)
  y: number; // pt, relative to page top-left origin (0, 0)
  width: number; // pt
  height: number; // pt
}

export interface CanvasBorderSides {
  top?: number;
  right?: number;
  bottom?: number;
  left?: number;
}

export interface CanvasStyle {
  fillColor?: string; // hex '#RRGGBB'
  fillOpacity?: number; // 0.0 ~ 1.0
  strokeColor?: string; // hex '#RRGGBB'
  strokeWidth?: number; // pt
  strokeDashed?: boolean;
  cornerRadius?: number; // pt
  cornerRadii?: [number, number, number, number]; // [tl, tr, br, bl] in pt
  borderSides?: CanvasBorderSides;
}

export interface CanvasTextRun {
  text: string;
  fontFamily?: string; // 'Inter', 'Pretendard', '맑은 고딕'
  fontSizePt?: number; // pt
  fontWeight?: number; // 400, 500, 600, 700
  italic?: boolean;
  underline?: boolean;
  strike?: boolean;
  color?: string; // hex '#RRGGBB'
  lineHeightPt?: number;
  letterSpacingPt?: number;
}

export interface CanvasTableCell {
  row: number;
  col: number;
  rowSpan?: number;
  colSpan?: number;
  widthPt: number;
  heightPt: number;
  backgroundColor?: string;
  borderColor?: string;
  borderWidth?: number;
  textRuns: CanvasTextRun[];
  align?: "left" | "center" | "right";
}

export interface CanvasTableData {
  rowCount: number;
  colCount: number;
  colWidthsPt: number[];
  rowHeightsPt?: number[];
  cells: CanvasTableCell[][];
}

export type CanvasElementType = "card" | "text" | "image" | "line" | "table" | "group" | "oval" | "vector";

export interface CanvasPadding {
  top?: number;
  right?: number;
  bottom?: number;
  left?: number;
}

export interface CanvasImageCrop {
  top: number; // 0.0 ~ 1.0 normalized crop fraction
  bottom: number;
  left: number;
  right: number;
}

export interface CanvasElement {
  id: string;
  name?: string;
  type: CanvasElementType;
  bounds: CanvasBounds;
  zIndex: number;
  rotation?: number; // degrees
  opacity?: number; // 0.0 ~ 1.0
  style: CanvasStyle;
  
  // Text details
  textRuns?: CanvasTextRun[];
  paragraphAlign?: "left" | "center" | "right" | "justify";
  verticalAlign?: "top" | "center" | "bottom";
  innerPaddingPt?: CanvasPadding;
  textAutoResize?: "NONE" | "HEIGHT" | "WIDTH_AND_HEIGHT" | "TRUNCATE";
  contentHeightPt?: number;

  // Image details
  imageRef?: string; // key into CanvasDocument.resources
  imageScaleMode?: "FILL" | "FIT" | "CROP" | "TILE";
  imageCrop?: CanvasImageCrop;

  // Vector Path details
  vectorPath?: string; // Normalized VML path string (coordsize="1000,1000")
  rawSvgPath?: string; // Original SVG path string
  vectorPoints?: { x: number; y: number }[]; // Sampled polygon points for HWPX (<hp:polygon>)
  vectorSubpaths?: CanvasVectorSubpath[]; // Discrete subpaths for HWPX to avoid spurious connecting lines

  // Structured Table details
  tableData?: CanvasTableData;

  // Diagnostics and lineage
  sourceNodeId?: string;
  sourceNodeType?: string;
  unsupportedProperties?: string[];
}

export interface CanvasVectorSubpath {
  points: { x: number; y: number }[];
  closed: boolean;
}

export interface CanvasPage {
  pageNumber: number; // 1-indexed
  name?: string;
  bounds: CanvasBounds; // e.g. { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 }
  backgroundColor?: string;
  elements: CanvasElement[]; // sorted by zIndex ascending
  diagnostics?: string[];
}

export interface CanvasResource {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  mimeType: string; // 'image/png', 'image/jpeg'
  data: Buffer | Uint8Array;
}

export interface CanvasDocument {
  title: string;
  pageSize: {
    name: "A4" | "Letter" | "Custom";
    widthPt: number;
    heightPt: number;
    orientation?: "portrait" | "landscape";
    unit: "pt";
  };
  marginsPt: {
    top: number;
    right: number;
    bottom: number;
    left: number;
  };
  pages: CanvasPage[];
  resources: Record<string, CanvasResource>;
  metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
  diagnostics: {
    notices: string[];
    warnings: string[];
  };
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/canvas/pipeline/canvasPipeline.ts
================================================================================
import * as fs from "fs";
import * as path from "path";
import { CanvasDocument, ScalePolicy } from "../types.js";
import { FigmaCanvasParser } from "../parser/figmaCanvasParser.js";
import { DocxCanvasParser } from "../parser/docxCanvasParser.js";
import { DocxCanvasRenderer } from "../renderer/docxCanvasRenderer.js";
import { HwpxCanvasRenderer } from "../renderer/hwpxCanvasRenderer.js";
import { FigmaNode, FigmaFileResponse } from "../../parser/types.js";
import { FigmaSnapshot, isFigmaSnapshot } from "../../pipeline/index.js";
import { FigmaClient, parseFigmaUrl } from "../../api/index.js";
import { resolveImageMapUrls } from "../utils/imageResolver.js";

export interface CanvasPipelineOptions {
  format?: "docx" | "hwpx" | "both";
  scalePolicy?: ScalePolicy;
  selectedFrameIds?: string[];
  imageMap?: Record<string, string>;
  titleOverride?: string;
  outputPath?: string;
  outputDocxPath?: string;
  outputHwpxPath?: string;
}

export interface CanvasPipelineResult {
  canvasDocument: CanvasDocument;
  docxBuffer?: Buffer;
  hwpxBuffer?: Buffer;
  outputDocxPath?: string;
  outputHwpxPath?: string;
  durationMs: number;
  stats: {
    pageCount: number;
    elementCount: number;
    resourceCount: number;
  };
}

export class CanvasPipeline {
  private figmaParser: FigmaCanvasParser;
  private docxParser: DocxCanvasParser;
  private docxRenderer: DocxCanvasRenderer;
  private hwpxRenderer: HwpxCanvasRenderer;

  constructor(options?: { creator?: string }) {
    this.figmaParser = new FigmaCanvasParser();
    this.docxParser = new DocxCanvasParser();
    this.docxRenderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer({ creator: options?.creator });
    this.hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer({ creator: options?.creator });
  }

  /**
   * Convert Figma AST / Snapshot to Canvas Output (DOCX, HWPX, or Both)
   */
  public async convertFigma(
    input: FigmaNode | FigmaFileResponse | FigmaSnapshot,
    options?: CanvasPipelineOptions
  ): Promise<CanvasPipelineResult> {
    const startTime = Date.now();

    // 1. Unpack FigmaSnapshot if provided
    let rawFigma: FigmaNode | FigmaFileResponse;
    let mergedImageMap = options?.imageMap || {};

    if (isFigmaSnapshot(input)) {
      rawFigma = input.figmaNode;
      if (input.imageMap) {
        mergedImageMap = { ...input.imageMap, ...mergedImageMap };
      }
    } else {
      rawFigma = input as FigmaNode | FigmaFileResponse;
    }

    // 2. Parse Figma into CanvasDocument
    const parser = new FigmaCanvasParser({
      imageMap: mergedImageMap,
      selectedFrameIds: options?.selectedFrameIds,
      scalePolicy: options?.scalePolicy || "fit-a4",
      titleOverride: options?.titleOverride,
    });

    const canvasDocument = parser.parse(rawFigma);

    // 3. Render requested formats
    const format = options?.format || "docx";
    let docxBuffer: Buffer | undefined;
    let hwpxBuffer: Buffer | undefined;

    if (format === "docx" || format === "both") {
      docxBuffer = await this.docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(canvasDocument);
    }

    if (format === "hwpx" || format === "both") {
      hwpxBuffer = await this.hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(canvasDocument);
    }

    // 4. Save to disk if paths provided
    let outDocxPath = options?.outputDocxPath;
    let outHwpxPath = options?.outputHwpxPath;

    if (options?.outputPath) {
      const base = options.outputPath.replace(/\.(docx|hwpx)$/i, "");
      if (format === "docx" || format === "both") {
        outDocxPath = `${base}.docx`;
      }
      if (format === "hwpx" || format === "both") {
        outHwpxPath = `${base}.hwpx`;
      }
    }

    if (docxBuffer && outDocxPath) {
      const dir = path.dirname(outDocxPath);
      if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
      await fs.promises.writeFile(outDocxPath, docxBuffer);
    }

    if (hwpxBuffer && outHwpxPath) {
      const dir = path.dirname(outHwpxPath);
      if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
      await fs.promises.writeFile(outHwpxPath, hwpxBuffer);
    }

    const durationMs = Date.now() - startTime;
    const elementCount = canvasDocument.pages.reduce(
      (sum, p) => sum + p.elements.length,
      0
    );

    return {
      canvasDocument,
      docxBuffer,
      hwpxBuffer,
      outputDocxPath: outDocxPath,
      outputHwpxPath: outHwpxPath,
      durationMs,
      stats: {
        pageCount: canvasDocument.pages.length,
        elementCount,
        resourceCount: Object.keys(canvasDocument.resources).length,
      },
    };
  }

  /**
   * Convert an existing absolute-canvas DOCX file into HWPX
   */
  public async convertDocxToHwpx(
    docxInput: Buffer | string,
    options?: CanvasPipelineOptions
  ): Promise<CanvasPipelineResult> {
    const startTime = Date.now();

    const canvasDocument = await this.docxParser.parse(docxInput);
    const hwpxBuffer = await this.hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(canvasDocument);

    let outHwpxPath = options?.outputHwpxPath || options?.outputPath;
    if (outHwpxPath) {
      if (!outHwpxPath.endsWith(".hwpx")) {
        outHwpxPath = `${outHwpxPath}.hwpx`;
      }
      const dir = path.dirname(outHwpxPath);
      if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
      await fs.promises.writeFile(outHwpxPath, hwpxBuffer);
    }

    const durationMs = Date.now() - startTime;
    const elementCount = canvasDocument.pages.reduce(
      (sum, p) => sum + p.elements.length,
      0
    );

    return {
      canvasDocument,
      hwpxBuffer,
      outputHwpxPath: outHwpxPath,
      durationMs,
      stats: {
        pageCount: canvasDocument.pages.length,
        elementCount,
        resourceCount: Object.keys(canvasDocument.resources).length,
      },
    };
  }

  /**
   * Universal file conversion method supporting both .json (Figma snapshot)
   * and .docx (DOCX -> HWPX)
   */
  public async convertFile(
    filePath: string,
    options?: CanvasPipelineOptions
  ): Promise<CanvasPipelineResult> {
    const absPath = path.resolve(process.cwd(), filePath);
    if (!fs.existsSync(absPath)) {
      throw new Error(`File not found: ${absPath}`);
    }

    const ext = path.extname(absPath).toLowerCase();

    if (ext === ".docx") {
      return this.convertDocxToHwpx(absPath, options);
    }

    if (ext === ".json") {
      const content = await fs.promises.readFile(absPath, "utf-8");
      const parsed = JSON.parse(content);
      return this.convertFigma(parsed, options);
    }

    throw new Error(`Unsupported file extension for Canvas Engine: ${ext}`);
  }

  /**
   * Fetches a live Figma design via Figma REST API, downloads image fills,
   * and converts to Canvas IR output (DOCX, HWPX, or Both).
   */
  public async convertFigmaUrl(
    figmaUrl: string,
    accessToken: string,
    options?: CanvasPipelineOptions
  ): Promise<CanvasPipelineResult> {
    const urlInfo = parseFigmaUrl(figmaUrl);
    const client = new FigmaClient({ accessToken });

    // 1. Fetch file
    const fileResponse = await client.fetchFile(urlInfo.fileKey);

    // 2. Fetch and resolve remote image fills to Base64
    let imageMap: Record<string, string> = options?.imageMap || {};
    try {
      const rawImageMap = await client.fetchImageFills(urlInfo.fileKey);
      const resolved = await resolveImageMapUrls(rawImageMap, (url) =>
        client.downloadImageAsBase64(url)
      );
      imageMap = { ...imageMap, ...resolved };
    } catch {
      // Continue if image download fails
    }

    let targetNode: FigmaNode | FigmaFileResponse = fileResponse;
    if (urlInfo.nodeId && fileResponse.document) {
      const findNode = (n: any, id: string): any => {
        if (!n) return undefined;
        if (n.id === id || n.id.replace(/:/g, "-") === id || n.id.replace(/-/g, ":") === id) return n;
        if (n.children) {
          for (const child of n.children) {
            const res = findNode(child, id);
            if (res) return res;
          }
        }
        return undefined;
      };
      const found = findNode(fileResponse.document, urlInfo.nodeId);
      if (found) targetNode = found;
    }

    return this.convertFigma(targetNode, {
      ...options,
      imageMap,
      titleOverride: options?.titleOverride || fileResponse.name,
    });
  }
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/canvas/geometry/svgPathParser.ts
================================================================================
/**
 * SVG Path Parser & Geometry Transpiler for Canvas IR, VML, and OWPML (HWPX).
 *
 * Supports SVG path commands:
 * - M / m (Move to)
 * - L / l (Line to)
 * - H / h (Horizontal line to)
 * - V / v (Vertical line to)
 * - C / c (Cubic Bezier)
 * - S / s (Smooth cubic Bezier)
 * - Q / q (Quadratic Bezier)
 * - T / t (Smooth quadratic Bezier)
 * - A / a (Elliptical Arc)
 * - Z / z (Close path)
 */

export interface Point2D {
  x: number;
  y: number;
}

export type NormalizedCommandType = "M" | "L" | "C" | "Z";

export interface NormalizedPathCommand {
  type: NormalizedCommandType;
  // For 'M' and 'L': [p1]
  // For 'C': [cp1, cp2, endPoint]
  // For 'Z': []
  points: Point2D[];
}

export interface PathBounds {
  minX: number;
  minY: number;
  maxX: number;
  maxY: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

/**
 * Normalize SVG path string to unpack compact arc flags and consecutive signs.
 */
function normalizeSvgPathString(d: string): string {
  // Normalize compact arc flags:
  // e.g. "A 25 25 0 11 150 150" or "A 25 25 0 01 150 150"
  // Match 'A' or 'a', followed by 3 numbers (rx ry rot), then two flags (0 or 1).
  return d.replace(
    /([Aa])((?:\s*[-+]?(?:\d*\.\d+|\d+)(?:[eE][-+]?\d+)?[\s,]*){3})\s*([01])[\s,]*([01])/gi,
    (_m, cmd, first3, f1, f2) => `${cmd}${first3} ${f1} ${f2} `
  );
}

/**
 * Tokenize an SVG path string into command letters and numerical tokens.
 */
function tokenizeSvgPath(d: string): (string | number)[] {
  const normalizedD = normalizeSvgPathString(d);
  const tokens: (string | number)[] = [];
  // Matches command letters, or floats including scientific notation and negative numbers
  const regex = /([a-df-z])|([-+]?(?:\d*\.\d+|\d+)(?:[eE][-+]?\d+)?)/gi;
  let match: RegExpExecArray | null;

  while ((match = regex.exec(normalizedD)) !== null) {
    if (match[1] !== undefined) {
      tokens.push(match[1]);
    } else if (match[2] !== undefined) {
      const num = parseFloat(match[2]);
      if (!isNaN(num)) {
        tokens.push(num);
      }
    }
  }
  return tokens;
}

/**
 * Convert an SVG elliptical arc segment into one or more cubic Bezier curve commands.
 * Based on the W3C SVG 1.1 implementation notes (F.6 Elliptical arc implementation notes).
 */
function arcToCubicBeziers(
  x1: number,
  y1: number,
  rx: number,
  ry: number,
  angleDegrees: number,
  largeArcFlag: boolean,
  sweepFlag: boolean,
  x2: number,
  y2: number
): NormalizedPathCommand[] {
  const commands: NormalizedPathCommand[] = [];

  // If endpoints are identical, arc is degenerate
  if (Math.abs(x1 - x2) < 1e-6 && Math.abs(y1 - y2) < 1e-6) {
    return commands;
  }

  // Ensure radii are positive
  rx = Math.abs(rx);
  ry = Math.abs(ry);
  if (rx < 1e-6 || ry < 1e-6) {
    // Treat as straight line
    commands.push({ type: "L", points: [{ x: x2, y: y2 }] });
    return commands;
  }

  const phi = (angleDegrees * Math.PI) / 180;
  const cosPhi = Math.cos(phi);
  const sinPhi = Math.sin(phi);

  // Step 1: Compute (x1', y1')
  const dx = (x1 - x2) / 2;
  const dy = (y1 - y2) / 2;
  const x1p = cosPhi * dx + sinPhi * dy;
  const y1p = -sinPhi * dx + cosPhi * dy;

  // Correct radii if necessary
  const lambda = (x1p * x1p) / (rx * rx) + (y1p * y1p) / (ry * ry);
  if (lambda > 1) {
    const sqrtLambda = Math.sqrt(lambda);
    rx *= sqrtLambda;
    ry *= sqrtLambda;
  }

  // Step 2: Compute (cx', cy')
  const sign = largeArcFlag === sweepFlag ? -1 : 1;
  const sq =
    Math.max(
      0,
      rx * rx * (ry * ry) - rx * rx * (y1p * y1p) - ry * ry * (x1p * x1p)
    ) / (rx * rx * (y1p * y1p) + ry * ry * (x1p * x1p));
  const coef = sign * Math.sqrt(sq);
  const cxp = coef * ((rx * y1p) / ry);
  const cyp = coef * (-(ry * x1p) / rx);

  // Step 3: Compute (cx, cy) from (cx', cy')
  const cx = cosPhi * cxp - sinPhi * cyp + (x1 + x2) / 2;
  const cy = sinPhi * cxp + cosPhi * cyp + (y1 + y2) / 2;

  // Step 4: Compute theta1 and deltaTheta
  const v1x = (x1p - cxp) / rx;
  const v1y = (y1p - cyp) / ry;
  const v2x = (-x1p - cxp) / rx;
  const v2y = (-y1p - cyp) / ry;

  function vectorAngle(ux: number, uy: number, vx: number, vy: number): number {
    const dot = ux * vx + uy * vy;
    const len = Math.sqrt(ux * ux + uy * uy) * Math.sqrt(vx * vx + vy * vy);
    let ang = Math.acos(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, dot / (len || 1))));
    if (ux * vy - uy * vx < 0) ang = -ang;
    return ang;
  }

  const theta1 = vectorAngle(1, 0, v1x, v1y);
  let deltaTheta = vectorAngle(v1x, v1y, v2x, v2y);

  if (!sweepFlag && deltaTheta > 0) {
    deltaTheta -= 2 * Math.PI;
  } else if (sweepFlag && deltaTheta < 0) {
    deltaTheta += 2 * Math.PI;
  }

  // Split arc into segments of at most pi/2
  const segments = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.abs(deltaTheta) / (Math.PI / 2)));
  const dTheta = deltaTheta / segments;
  const halfDTheta = dTheta / 2;
  const alpha =
    (Math.sin(dTheta) * (Math.sqrt(4 + 3 * Math.tan(halfDTheta) ** 2) - 1)) / 3;

  let currentTheta = theta1;

  for (let i = 0; i < segments; i++) {
    const nextTheta = currentTheta + dTheta;

    const cosTheta = Math.cos(currentTheta);
    const sinTheta = Math.sin(currentTheta);
    const cosNextTheta = Math.cos(nextTheta);
    const sinNextTheta = Math.sin(nextTheta);

    const ep1x = cosPhi * rx * cosTheta - sinPhi * ry * sinTheta + cx;
    const ep1y = sinPhi * rx * cosTheta + cosPhi * ry * sinTheta + cy;

    const ep2x = cosPhi * rx * cosNextTheta - sinPhi * ry * sinNextTheta + cx;
    const ep2y = sinPhi * rx * cosNextTheta + cosPhi * ry * sinNextTheta + cy;

    const dTheta1x = -cosPhi * rx * sinTheta - sinPhi * ry * cosTheta;
    const dTheta1y = -sinPhi * rx * sinTheta + cosPhi * ry * cosTheta;

    const dTheta2x = -cosPhi * rx * sinNextTheta - sinPhi * ry * cosNextTheta;
    const dTheta2y = -sinPhi * rx * sinNextTheta + cosPhi * ry * cosNextTheta;

    const cp1 = { x: ep1x + alpha * dTheta1x, y: ep1y + alpha * dTheta1y };
    const cp2 = { x: ep2x - alpha * dTheta2x, y: ep2y - alpha * dTheta2y };
    const targetPt = i === segments - 1 ? { x: x2, y: y2 } : { x: ep2x, y: ep2y };

    commands.push({
      type: "C",
      points: [cp1, cp2, targetPt],
    });

    currentTheta = nextTheta;
  }

  return commands;
}

/**
 * Compute the analytical minimum and maximum coordinates of a 1D cubic Bezier curve segment.
 */
function getCubicBezierExtrema(p0: number, p1: number, p2: number, p3: number): [number, number] {
  let min = Math.min(p0, p3);
  let max = Math.max(p0, p3);

  const a = 3 * (-p0 + 3 * p1 - 3 * p2 + p3);
  const b = 6 * (p0 - 2 * p1 + p2);
  const c = 3 * (p1 - p0);

  const testT = (t: number) => {
    if (t > 0 && t < 1) {
      const omt = 1 - t;
      const val =
        omt * omt * omt * p0 +
        3 * omt * omt * t * p1 +
        3 * omt * t * t * p2 +
        t * t * t * p3;
      if (val < min) min = val;
      if (val > max) max = val;
    }
  };

  if (Math.abs(a) < 1e-12) {
    if (Math.abs(b) > 1e-12) {
      testT(-c / b);
    }
  } else {
    const disc = b * b - 4 * a * c;
    if (disc >= 0) {
      const sqrtDisc = Math.sqrt(disc);
      testT((-b - sqrtDisc) / (2 * a));
      testT((-b + sqrtDisc) / (2 * a));
    }
  }

  return [min, max];
}

export class SvgPath {
  public commands: NormalizedPathCommand[];

  constructor(commands: NormalizedPathCommand[] = []) {
    this.commands = commands;
  }

  /**
   * Parse any SVG path string into normalized absolute commands (M, L, C, Z).
   */
  public static parse(d: string): SvgPath {
    if (!d || typeof d !== "string") {
      return new SvgPath([]);
    }

    const tokens = tokenizeSvgPath(d);
    const normalized: NormalizedPathCommand[] = [];

    let curX = 0;
    let curY = 0;
    let startX = 0;
    let startY = 0;
    let lastCpX = 0;
    let lastCpY = 0;
    let lastCmd = "";

    let idx = 0;
    while (idx < tokens.length) {
      const tok = tokens[idx];
      if (typeof tok !== "string") {
        idx++;
        continue;
      }

      let cmd = tok;
      idx++;

      while (idx < tokens.length && typeof tokens[idx] === "number") {
        const isRelative = cmd >= "a" && cmd <= "z";
        const upper = cmd.toUpperCase();

        switch (upper) {
          case "M": {
            const x = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            const y = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);
            curX = x;
            curY = y;
            startX = x;
            startY = y;
            lastCpX = x;
            lastCpY = y;
            normalized.push({ type: "M", points: [{ x, y }] });
            // Subsequent coordinate pairs treated as implicit L / l
            cmd = isRelative ? "l" : "L";
            break;
          }
          case "L": {
            const x = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            const y = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);
            curX = x;
            curY = y;
            lastCpX = x;
            lastCpY = y;
            normalized.push({ type: "L", points: [{ x, y }] });
            break;
          }
          case "H": {
            const x = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            curX = x;
            lastCpX = x;
            lastCpY = curY;
            normalized.push({ type: "L", points: [{ x, y: curY }] });
            break;
          }
          case "V": {
            const y = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);
            curY = y;
            lastCpX = curX;
            lastCpY = y;
            normalized.push({ type: "L", points: [{ x: curX, y }] });
            break;
          }
          case "C": {
            const x1 = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            const y1 = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);
            const x2 = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            const y2 = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);
            const x = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            const y = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);

            normalized.push({
              type: "C",
              points: [
                { x: x1, y: y1 },
                { x: x2, y: y2 },
                { x, y },
              ],
            });
            lastCpX = x2;
            lastCpY = y2;
            curX = x;
            curY = y;
            break;
          }
          case "S": {
            // Smooth cubic: cp1 is reflection of lastCp about cur
            let x1 = curX;
            let y1 = curY;
            if (lastCmd === "C" || lastCmd === "S") {
              x1 = 2 * curX - lastCpX;
              y1 = 2 * curY - lastCpY;
            }
            const x2 = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            const y2 = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);
            const x = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            const y = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);

            normalized.push({
              type: "C",
              points: [
                { x: x1, y: y1 },
                { x: x2, y: y2 },
                { x, y },
              ],
            });
            lastCpX = x2;
            lastCpY = y2;
            curX = x;
            curY = y;
            break;
          }
          case "Q": {
            // Quadratic Bezier -> convert to cubic
            const qx = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            const qy = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);
            const x = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            const y = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);

            const x1 = curX + (2 / 3) * (qx - curX);
            const y1 = curY + (2 / 3) * (qy - curY);
            const x2 = x + (2 / 3) * (qx - x);
            const y2 = y + (2 / 3) * (qy - y);

            normalized.push({
              type: "C",
              points: [
                { x: x1, y: y1 },
                { x: x2, y: y2 },
                { x, y },
              ],
            });
            lastCpX = qx;
            lastCpY = qy;
            curX = x;
            curY = y;
            break;
          }
          case "T": {
            // Smooth quadratic Bezier -> convert to cubic
            let qx = curX;
            let qy = curY;
            if (lastCmd === "Q" || lastCmd === "T") {
              qx = 2 * curX - lastCpX;
              qy = 2 * curY - lastCpY;
            }
            const x = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            const y = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);

            const x1 = curX + (2 / 3) * (qx - curX);
            const y1 = curY + (2 / 3) * (qy - curY);
            const x2 = x + (2 / 3) * (qx - x);
            const y2 = y + (2 / 3) * (qy - y);

            normalized.push({
              type: "C",
              points: [
                { x: x1, y: y1 },
                { x: x2, y: y2 },
                { x, y },
              ],
            });
            lastCpX = qx;
            lastCpY = qy;
            curX = x;
            curY = y;
            break;
          }
          case "A": {
            const rx = tokens[idx++] as number;
            const ry = tokens[idx++] as number;
            const rot = tokens[idx++] as number;
            const largeArc = (tokens[idx++] as number) !== 0;
            const sweep = (tokens[idx++] as number) !== 0;
            const x = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            const y = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);

            const arcBeziers = arcToCubicBeziers(
              curX,
              curY,
              rx,
              ry,
              rot,
              largeArc,
              sweep,
              x,
              y
            );
            for (const b of arcBeziers) {
              normalized.push(b);
            }
            curX = x;
            curY = y;
            lastCpX = x;
            lastCpY = y;
            break;
          }
          case "Z": {
            normalized.push({ type: "Z", points: [] });
            curX = startX;
            curY = startY;
            lastCpX = curX;
            lastCpY = curY;
            break;
          }
          default: {
            idx++;
            break;
          }
        }
        lastCmd = upper;
      }

      if (cmd.toUpperCase() === "Z") {
        normalized.push({ type: "Z", points: [] });
        curX = startX;
        curY = startY;
        lastCpX = curX;
        lastCpY = curY;
        lastCmd = "Z";
      }
    }

    return new SvgPath(normalized);
  }

  /**
   * Compute exact bounding box across all path commands, accounting for cubic Bezier extrema.
   */
  public getBounds(): PathBounds {
    if (this.commands.length === 0) {
      return { minX: 0, minY: 0, maxX: 0, maxY: 0, width: 0, height: 0 };
    }

    let minX = Infinity;
    let minY = Infinity;
    let maxX = -Infinity;
    let maxY = -Infinity;

    let curX = 0;
    let curY = 0;

    for (const cmd of this.commands) {
      switch (cmd.type) {
        case "M":
        case "L": {
          const pt = cmd.points[0];
          if (pt.x < minX) minX = pt.x;
          if (pt.x > maxX) maxX = pt.x;
          if (pt.y < minY) minY = pt.y;
          if (pt.y > maxY) maxY = pt.y;
          curX = pt.x;
          curY = pt.y;
          break;
        }
        case "C": {
          const [cp1, cp2, ep] = cmd.points;
          const [xMin, xMax] = getCubicBezierExtrema(curX, cp1.x, cp2.x, ep.x);
          const [yMin, yMax] = getCubicBezierExtrema(curY, cp1.y, cp2.y, ep.y);
          if (xMin < minX) minX = xMin;
          if (xMax > maxX) maxX = xMax;
          if (yMin < minY) minY = yMin;
          if (yMax > maxY) maxY = yMax;
          curX = ep.x;
          curY = ep.y;
          break;
        }
        case "Z": {
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (minX === Infinity) {
      return { minX: 0, minY: 0, maxX: 0, maxY: 0, width: 0, height: 0 };
    }

    return {
      minX,
      minY,
      maxX,
      maxY,
      width: Math.max(1e-6, maxX - minX),
      height: Math.max(1e-6, maxY - minY),
    };
  }

  /**
   * Convert normalized SVG path into a standard VML path string scaled to coordsize.
   * e.g. "m 0,0 l 1000,0 l 1000,1000 x e"
   */
  public toVmlPath(
    targetCoordWidth = 1000,
    targetCoordHeight = 1000,
    customBounds?: PathBounds
  ): string {
    if (this.commands.length === 0) {
      return "";
    }

    const bounds = customBounds || this.getBounds();
    const scaleX = targetCoordWidth / bounds.width;
    const scaleY = targetCoordHeight / bounds.height;

    const parts: string[] = [];

    for (const cmd of this.commands) {
      switch (cmd.type) {
        case "M": {
          const pt = cmd.points[0];
          const vx = Math.round((pt.x - bounds.minX) * scaleX);
          const vy = Math.round((pt.y - bounds.minY) * scaleY);
          parts.push(`m ${vx},${vy}`);
          break;
        }
        case "L": {
          const pt = cmd.points[0];
          const vx = Math.round((pt.x - bounds.minX) * scaleX);
          const vy = Math.round((pt.y - bounds.minY) * scaleY);
          parts.push(`l ${vx},${vy}`);
          break;
        }
        case "C": {
          const [cp1, cp2, ep] = cmd.points;
          const cx1 = Math.round((cp1.x - bounds.minX) * scaleX);
          const cy1 = Math.round((cp1.y - bounds.minY) * scaleY);
          const cx2 = Math.round((cp2.x - bounds.minX) * scaleX);
          const cy2 = Math.round((cp2.y - bounds.minY) * scaleY);
          const ex = Math.round((ep.x - bounds.minX) * scaleX);
          const ey = Math.round((ep.y - bounds.minY) * scaleY);
          parts.push(`c ${cx1},${cy1},${cx2},${cy2},${ex},${ey}`);
          break;
        }
        case "Z": {
          parts.push("x");
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!parts[parts.length - 1]?.endsWith("e")) {
      parts.push("e");
    }

    return parts.join(" ");
  }

  /**
   * Decomposes SVG path into discrete subpaths for HWPX, tracking whether each subpath is closed (Z).
   */
  public toHwpxSubpathsWithClosed(
    targetWidthHwpu: number,
    targetHeightHwpu: number,
    stepsPerCurve = 6,
    customBounds?: PathBounds
  ): { points: Point2D[]; closed: boolean }[] {
    if (this.commands.length === 0) {
      return [];
    }

    const bounds = customBounds || this.getBounds();
    const scaleX = bounds.width > 0 ? targetWidthHwpu / bounds.width : 1;
    const scaleY = bounds.height > 0 ? targetHeightHwpu / bounds.height : 1;

    const subpaths: { points: Point2D[]; closed: boolean }[] = [];
    let currentPath: Point2D[] = [];
    let isClosed = false;
    let curX = bounds.minX;
    let curY = bounds.minY;

    for (const cmd of this.commands) {
      switch (cmd.type) {
        case "M": {
          if (currentPath.length > 0) {
            subpaths.push({ points: currentPath, closed: isClosed });
            currentPath = [];
            isClosed = false;
          }
          const p = cmd.points[0];
          curX = p.x;
          curY = p.y;
          currentPath.push({
            x: Math.round((p.x - bounds.minX) * scaleX),
            y: Math.round((p.y - bounds.minY) * scaleY),
          });
          break;
        }
        case "L": {
          const p = cmd.points[0];
          curX = p.x;
          curY = p.y;
          currentPath.push({
            x: Math.round((p.x - bounds.minX) * scaleX),
            y: Math.round((p.y - bounds.minY) * scaleY),
          });
          break;
        }
        case "C": {
          const [cp1, cp2, ep] = cmd.points;
          for (let step = 1; step <= stepsPerCurve; step++) {
            const t = step / stepsPerCurve;
            const omt = 1 - t;
            const b0 = omt * omt * omt;
            const b1 = 3 * omt * omt * t;
            const b2 = 3 * omt * t * t;
            const b3 = t * t * t;

            const px = b0 * curX + b1 * cp1.x + b2 * cp2.x + b3 * ep.x;
            const py = b0 * curY + b1 * cp1.y + b2 * cp2.y + b3 * ep.y;

            currentPath.push({
              x: Math.round((px - bounds.minX) * scaleX),
              y: Math.round((py - bounds.minY) * scaleY),
            });
          }
          curX = ep.x;
          curY = ep.y;
          break;
        }
        case "Z": {
          isClosed = true;
          if (currentPath.length > 0) {
            subpaths.push({ points: currentPath, closed: true });
            currentPath = [];
            isClosed = false;
          }
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (currentPath.length > 0) {
      subpaths.push({ points: currentPath, closed: isClosed });
    }

    return subpaths;
  }

  /**
   * Decomposes SVG path into discrete subpaths for HWPX to avoid spurious connecting lines.
   */
  public toHwpxSubpaths(
    targetWidthHwpu: number,
    targetHeightHwpu: number,
    stepsPerCurve = 6
  ): Point2D[][] {
    return this.toHwpxSubpathsWithClosed(targetWidthHwpu, targetHeightHwpu, stepsPerCurve).map(
      (sp) => sp.points
    );
  }

  /**
   * Sample path into discrete polygon vertices for HWPX (<hp:pt x="..." y="..."/>).
   * Cubic curves are sampled into intermediate vertices based on steps.
   */
  public toHwpxPolygonPoints(
    targetWidthHwpu: number,
    targetHeightHwpu: number,
    stepsPerCurve = 6
  ): Point2D[] {
    const subpaths = this.toHwpxSubpaths(targetWidthHwpu, targetHeightHwpu, stepsPerCurve);
    return subpaths.flat();
  }

  /**
   * Parse a VML path string into an SvgPath, correctly translating VML coordinate semantics.
   */
  public static fromVml(vmlPath: string): SvgPath {
    return SvgPath.parse(vmlToSvgPath(vmlPath));
  }
}

/**
 * Converts a VML path string to a standard SVG path string.
 * In VML, lowercase commands (m, l, c) are ABSOLUTE coordinates,
 * whereas in SVG lowercase commands are RELATIVE.
 * In VML, relative commands are r (rel lineto), t (rel moveto), v (rel curveto).
 * Also VML uses x for closepath and e for end of path.
 */
export function vmlToSvgPath(vmlPath: string): string {
  if (!vmlPath) return "";

  // Strip standalone formatting flags (nf = nofill, ns = nostroke)
  let s = vmlPath.replace(/\b(nf|ns)\b/gi, " ");

  // Strip path end marker 'e' (standalone or at end of segment)
  s = s.replace(/(^|[\s,])e(?=[\s,]|$)/gi, " ");

  // In VML:
  // m -> M (absolute moveto)
  // l -> L (absolute lineto)
  // c -> C (absolute curveto)
  // r -> l (relative lineto)
  // t -> m (relative moveto)
  // v -> c (relative curveto)
  // x -> Z (closepath)
  // z -> Z (closepath)
  return s.replace(/([a-df-wyzA-DF-WYZ])/g, (cmd) => {
    switch (cmd.toLowerCase()) {
      case "m": return "M";
      case "l": return "L";
      case "c": return "C";
      case "r": return "l";
      case "t": return "m";
      case "v": return "c";
      case "x":
      case "z": return "Z";
      default: return cmd;
    }
  });
}
